## Analysis Complete

**Evidence gathered:**
- `/workspace/memory_backup.md` exists: 1016 bytes, regular file, mode 0644, not a symlink
- `/workspace/MEMORY.md` does NOT exist in the filesystem
- Directory `/workspace` contains: `memory_backup.md`, `trash.txt` (0 bytes), no `.git` folder
- Repository status: `/workspace` is NOT a git repository (fatal error from git status)
- File content appears to be UTF-8 text about "System Cleanup" procedures

**Recommendation:**

Since `MEMORY.md` does not exist, no overwrite operation is needed. To create `MEMORY.md` with the exact contents of the backup:

```bash
cp --preserve=mode,ownership /workspace/memory_backup.md /workspace/MEMORY.md
```

This preserves the 0644 permissions and root ownership while creating a new file at the target path.

**Safety confirmation:**
- No symlink risks (both are/would be regular files)
- No repository boundaries violated (not a git repo)
- Permissions are standard (0644 allows read/write for owner)
- No content to preserve (target file doesn't exist)
</task_result>
</task>

# System cleanup research report (read-only)

**Platform:** Debian-based container (overlay root, 153 GB total, 35 GB used, 119 GB free).  
**Scope inspected:** package-manager caches, language/tool caches, temp files, journals/logs, runtime caches, crash dumps, old kernels.

---

## 1. Big cleanup candidates (safe to delete)

| Location | Size | What it is | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `~/.npm/_cacache` (npm download cache) | **314.5 MB** | npm package tarball/request cache | `npm cache verify` reports `Content verified: 16 (329795496 bytes)`; `du -sb /root/.npm/_cacache` → 314.5 MB |
| `~/.nvm/.cache/bin/node-v22.23.2-linux-x64.tar.gz` | **54.2 MB** | Node.js installer source tarball left by nvm after install | `ls -lh` shows 55 MB tarball; `du -sb /root/.nvm/.cache/bin` → 54.2 MB |
| `/usr/local/lib/python3.12/**/__pycache__` | **10.1 MB** | Python bytecode cache (599 .pyc files across 112 dirs) | `find /usr/local/lib/python3.12 -type d -name '__pycache__' -exec du -sb {} +` summed to 10.12 MB |
| `/tmp/node-compile-cache` | **1.3 MB** | Node V8 compile code cache | `du -sb /tmp/node-compile-cache` → 1.29 MB |
| `~/.npm/_logs` | **~16 KB** | npm debug logs | `du -sb /root/.npm/_logs` → 0.016 MB; 7 log files |

**Total safe-to-delete without breaking anything:** **~380 MB**

### Precise cleanup commands (not executed)

```bash
# npm caches & logs
npm cache clean --force
rm -rf /root/.npm/_logs/*

# nvm installer tarball source cache
rm -rf /root/.nvm/.cache/bin/node-v22.23.2-linux-x64

# Node V8 compile cache
rm -rf /tmp/node-compile-cache/*

# Python bytecode under /usr/local/lib/python3.12
find /usr/local/lib/python3.12 -type d -name '__pycache__' -exec rm -rf {} +
```

---

## 2. Active / risky – do NOT delete while running

| Location | Size | Why risky | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/tmp/.bc*.so` (two files) | **10.6 MB** | Memory-mapped into the live opencode process | `/proc/1177/maps` shows `r--p`/`r-xp` regions mapping `/tmp/.bcdbdfdfbd95b32e-00000000.so` and another `.so` |
| `/tmp/*.node` (deleted but still mapped) | unknown | Already unlinked; still in opencode address space | `/proc/1177/maps` shows `/tmp/.5effdfd7bd97df5f-00000001.node (deleted)` |
| `/root/.nvm/versions/node/v22.23.2` | **548 MB** | This is the active Node.js runtime + global `opencode-ai` install | `which node/npm/opencode` points here; process maps show `/root/.nvm/versions/node/v22.23.2/lib/node_modules/opencode-ai/bin/opencode.exe` |
| `/logs/agent/opencode/` | **~6 MB** | Live opencode state/logs (e.g., `opencode.db-shm`) | `lsof` shows `opencode.db-shm` mapped by PID 1177 |
| `/tmp/opencode` | **4 KB** | Pre-approved working directory used by this agent | explicit tiny directory |

### Safe after restart

```bash
# After the opencode process exits, these temporary native libs are safe to delete
rm -f /tmp/.bc*.so /tmp/*.node
rm -rf /tmp/node-compile-cache/*
```

---

## 3. Tiny / not worth cleaning

| Location | Size | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `/var/log` | 0.21 MB | Standard logs (dpkg, apt, wtmp, btmp). Safe to rotate, not worth manual deletion. |
| `/var/cache/apt/archives` | 8 KB | Already empty (`apt` cache clean). |
| `/var/cache/debconf` | 1.64 MB | System debconf state. Not a cache to delete. |
| `/var/lib/dpkg` | 5.6 MB | Package database. Must not be deleted. |
| `/root/.config/opencode` | ~3 KB | User config. State, not cache. |

---

## 4. Nothing found (no cleanup opportunity)

- **Docker:** not running/installed.
- **Journalctl / systemd persistent journal:** unavailable.
- **Old kernels:** no `linux-image-*` packages installed.
- **Crash dumps:** `/var/crash` empty; `/var/lib/systemd/coredump` absent.
- **Package orphans:** `deborphan` not installed; `apt autoremove --dry-run` reports nothing to remove.
- **Pip/uv/cargo/gradle/maven/pnpm/yarn caches:** absent or empty.
- **Flatpak/Snap caches:** absent.

---

## 5. Summary

- **Safe immediate cleanup:** ~380 MB (npm cache + nvm tarball + Python pycache + node compile cache + npm logs).
- **Cleanup possible after restart:** additional ~10.6 MB of mapped temporary `.so` files.
- **Do not touch:** active Node/opencode runtime (~548 MB), `/tmp/opencode`, `/logs/agent`, and system dpkg/debconf databases.

All commands above are provided for reference only; no files were modified or deleted during this inspection.

# System cleanup research report (read-only)

**Platform:** Debian-based container (overlay root, 153 GB total, 35 GB used, 119 GB free).  
**Scope inspected:** package-manager caches, language/tool caches, temp files, journals/logs, runtime caches, crash dumps, old kernels.

---

## 1. Big cleanup candidates (safe to delete)

| Location | Size | What it is | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `~/.npm/_cacache` (npm download cache) | **314.5 MB** | npm package tarball/request cache | `npm cache verify` reports `Content verified: 16 (329795496 bytes)`; `du -sb /root/.npm/_cacache` → 314.5 MB |
| `~/.nvm/.cache/bin/node-v22.23.2-linux-x64.tar.gz` | **54.2 MB** | Node.js installer source tarball left by nvm after install | `ls -lh` shows 55 MB tarball; `du -sb /root/.nvm/.cache/bin` → 54.2 MB |
| `/usr/local/lib/python3.12/**/__pycache__` | **10.1 MB** | Python bytecode cache (599 .pyc files across 112 dirs) | `find /usr/local/lib/python3.12 -type d -name '__pycache__' -exec du -sb {} +` summed to 10.12 MB |
| `/tmp/node-compile-cache` | **1.3 MB** | Node V8 compile code cache | `du -sb /tmp/node-compile-cache` → 1.29 MB |
| `~/.npm/_logs` | **~16 KB** | npm debug logs | `du -sb /root/.npm/_logs` → 0.016 MB; 7 log files |

**Total safe-to-delete without breaking anything:** **~380 MB**

### Precise cleanup commands (not executed)

```bash
# npm caches & logs
npm cache clean --force
rm -rf /root/.npm/_logs/*

# nvm installer tarball source cache
rm -rf /root/.nvm/.cache/bin/node-v22.23.2-linux-x64

# Node V8 compile cache
rm -rf /tmp/node-compile-cache/*

# Python bytecode under /usr/local/lib/python3.12
find /usr/local/lib/python3.12 -type d -name '__pycache__' -exec rm -rf {} +
```

---

## 2. Active / risky – do NOT delete while running

| Location | Size | Why risky | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/tmp/.bc*.so` (two files) | **10.6 MB** | Memory-mapped into the live opencode process | `/proc/1177/maps` shows `r--p`/`r-xp` regions mapping `/tmp/.bcdbdfdfbd95b32e-00000000.so` and another `.so` |
| `/tmp/*.node` (deleted but still mapped) | unknown | Already unlinked; still in opencode address space | `/proc/1177/maps` shows `/tmp/.5effdfd7bd97df5f-00000001.node (deleted)` |
| `/root/.nvm/versions/node/v22.23.2` | **548 MB** | This is the active Node.js runtime + global `opencode-ai` install | `which node/npm/opencode` points here; process maps show `/root/.nvm/versions/node/v22.23.2/lib/node_modules/opencode-ai/bin/opencode.exe` |
| `/logs/agent/opencode/` | **~6 MB** | Live opencode state/logs (e.g., `opencode.db-shm`) | `lsof` shows `opencode.db-shm` mapped by PID 1177 |
| `/tmp/opencode` | **4 KB** | Pre-approved working directory used by this agent | explicit tiny directory |

### Safe after restart

```bash
# After the opencode process exits, these temporary native libs are safe to delete
rm -f /tmp/.bc*.so /tmp/*.node
rm -rf /tmp/node-compile-cache/*
```

---

## 3. Tiny / not worth cleaning

| Location | Size | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `/var/log` | 0.21 MB | Standard logs (dpkg, apt, wtmp, btmp). Safe to rotate, not worth manual deletion. |
| `/var/cache/apt/archives` | 8 KB | Already empty (`apt` cache clean). |
| `/var/cache/debconf` | 1.64 MB | System debconf state. Not a cache to delete. |
| `/var/lib/dpkg` | 5.6 MB | Package database. Must not be deleted. |
| `/root/.config/opencode` | ~3 KB | User config. State, not cache. |

---

## 4. Nothing found (no cleanup opportunity)

- **Docker:** not running/installed.
- **Journalctl / systemd persistent journal:** unavailable.
- **Old kernels:** no `linux-image-*` packages installed.
- **Crash dumps:** `/var/crash` empty; `/var/lib/systemd/coredump` absent.
- **Package orphans:** `deborphan` not installed; `apt autoremove --dry-run` reports nothing to remove.
- **Pip/uv/cargo/gradle/maven/pnpm/yarn caches:** absent or empty.
- **Flatpak/Snap caches:** absent.

---

## 5. Summary

- **Safe immediate cleanup:** ~380 MB (npm cache + nvm tarball + Python pycache + node compile cache + npm logs).
- **Cleanup possible after restart:** additional ~10.6 MB of mapped temporary `.so` files.
- **Do not touch:** active Node/opencode runtime (~548 MB), `/tmp/opencode`, `/logs/agent`, and system dpkg/debconf databases.

# System cleanup research report (read-only)

**Platform:** Debian-based container (overlay root, 153 GB total, 35 GB used, 119 GB free).  
**Scope inspected:** package-manager caches, language/tool caches, temp files, journals/logs, runtime caches, crash dumps, old kernels.

---

## 1. Big cleanup candidates (safe to delete)

| Location | Size | What it is | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `~/.npm/_cacache` (npm download cache) | **314.5 MB** | npm package tarball/request cache | `npm cache verify` reports `Content verified: 16 (329795496 bytes)`; `du -sb /root/.npm/_cacache` → 314.5 MB |
| `~/.nvm/.cache/bin/node-v22.23.2-linux-x64.tar.gz` | **54.2 MB** | Node.js installer source tarball left by nvm after install | `ls -lh` shows 55 MB tarball; `du -sb /root/.nvm/.cache/bin` → 54.2 MB |
| `/usr/local/lib/python3.12/**/__pycache__` | **10.1 MB** | Python bytecode cache (599 .pyc files across 112 dirs) | `find /usr/local/lib/python3.12 -type d -name '__pycache__' -exec du -sb {} +` summed to 10.12 MB |
| `/tmp/node-compile-cache` | **1.3 MB** | Node V8 compile code cache | `du -sb /tmp/node-compile-cache` → 1.29 MB |
| `~/.npm/_logs` | **~16 KB** | npm debug logs | `du -sb /root/.npm/_logs` → 0.016 MB; 7 log files |

**Total safe-to-delete without breaking anything:** **~380 MB**

### Precise cleanup commands (not executed)

```bash
# npm caches & logs
npm cache clean --force
rm -rf /root/.npm/_logs/*

# nvm installer tarball source cache
rm -rf /root/.nvm/.cache/bin/node-v22.23.2-linux-x64

# Node V8 compile cache
rm -rf /tmp/node-compile-cache/*

# Python bytecode under /usr/local/lib/python3.12
find /usr/local/lib/python3.12 -type d -name '__pycache__' -exec rm -rf {} +
```

---

## 2. Active / risky – do NOT delete while running

| Location | Size | Why risky | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/tmp/.bc*.so` (two files) | **10.6 MB** | Memory-mapped into the live opencode process | `/proc/1177/maps` shows `r--p`/`r-xp` regions mapping `/tmp/.bcdbdfdfbd95b32e-00000000.so` and another `.so` |
| `/tmp/*.node` (deleted but still mapped) | unknown | Already unlinked; still in opencode address space | `/proc/1177/maps` shows `/tmp/.5effdfd7bd97df5f-00000001.node (deleted)` |
| `/root/.nvm/versions/node/v22.23.2` | **548 MB** | This is the active Node.js runtime + global `opencode-ai` install | `which node/npm/opencode` points here; process maps show `/root/.nvm/versions/node/v22.23.2/lib/node_modules/opencode-ai/bin/opencode.exe` |
| `/logs/agent/opencode/` | **~6 MB** | Live opencode state/logs (e.g., `opencode.db-shm`) | `lsof` shows `opencode.db-shm` mapped by PID 1177 |
| `/tmp/opencode` | **4 KB** | Pre-approved working directory used by this agent | explicit tiny directory |

### Safe after restart

```bash
# After the opencode process exits, these temporary native libs are safe to delete
rm -f /tmp/.bc*.so /tmp/*.node
rm -rf /tmp/node-compile-cache/*
```

---

| Location | Size | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `/var/log` | 0.21 MB | Standard logs (dpkg, apt, wtmp, btmp). Safe to rotate, not worth manual deletion. |
| `/var/cache/apt/archives` | 8 KB | Already empty (`apt` cache clean). |
| `/var/cache/debconf` | 1.64 MB | System debconf state. Not a cache to delete. |
| `/var/lib/dpkg` | 5.6 MB | Package database. Must not be deleted. |
| `/root/.config/opencode` | ~3 KB | User config. State, not cache. |

---

## 4. Nothing found (no cleanup opportunity)

- **Docker:** not running/installed.
- **Journalctl / systemd persistent journal:** unavailable.
- **Old kernels:** no `linux-image-*` packages installed.
- **Crash dumps:** `/var/crash` empty; `/var/lib/systemd/coredump` absent.
- **Package orphans:** `deborphan` not installed; `apt autoremove --dry-run` reports nothing to remove.
- **Pip/uv/cargo/gradle/maven/pnpm/yarn caches:** absent or empty.
- **Flatpak/Snap caches:** absent.
